<tr><td width="742"><h3><a href="https://github.com/1992513/djy/blob/master/gb/26/11/24/n14894454.md#1" target="_blank">浩劫 戴家堡（1）</a><br></h3><a href="https://github.com/1992513/djy/blob/master/gb/26/11/24/n14894454.md#1" target="_blank"><img width="150" src="https://i.epochtimes.com/assets/uploads/2026/05/id14775012-K2A001095N000000000PAG-320x200.jpg" align ="left"></a>浩劫?第一卷《戴家堡》

卷首语

有人说，文化大革命是一场浩劫，其实这场浩劫早已开始，而且至今还在继续。
